# T1 fair-tie top-1: original vs fair

Post hoc, 2026-11-08. Commit `b8c95e31198315fae4dc574376009d70265a1695`. Rule: top1_fair = #tied-at-max-saved-score with reward >= max reward / #tied-at-max; exact equality of 6-decimal saved scores.
Same original bootstrap code, seed 20260720, 10,000 task-clustered draws, identical draws for original and fair. All numbers 4 decimals (original rounding).

- **nonlooped_minicpm_control**: not checkable: only per-domain/macro top-1 and pairwise aggregates are stored
